公司簡介


Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company”                舜 宇 光 學 科 技（ 集 團 ）有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」或
or “Sunny Optical Technology”, together with its subsidiaries, the             「舜宇光學科技」，連同其附屬公司「本集團」）
“Group”) (Stock Code: 2382.HK) is a global leading integrated optical          （股份代號：2382. H K）為一間全球領先的綜
components and products manufacturer. The Group is principally                 合光學零件及產品生產商。本集團主要從事設
engaged in the design, research and development (the “R&D”),                   計、研究與開發（「研發」）、生產及銷售光學及
manufacture and sales of optical and optical-related products. Such            光學相關產品。該等產品包括光學零件（例如
products include optical components (such as vehicle lens sets,                車載鏡頭、車載激光雷達（「激光雷達」）光學部
optical parts of vehicle light detection and ranging (“LiDAR”), virtual        件、虛擬現實（「VR」）感知及交互鏡頭、手機
reality (“VR”) perception and interaction lens sets, handset lens sets,        鏡頭、玻璃球面和非球面鏡片及其他光學零部
glass spherical and aspherical lenses and other optical components)            件）（「光學零件」）、光電產品（例如車載模組、
(the “Optical Components”), optoelectronic products (such as vehicle           VR視覺模組、手機攝像模組、機器人視覺模塊
modules, VR visual modules, handset camera modules, robotic vision             及其他光電模組）     （「光電產品」）及光學儀器（例

equipment and microscopes) (the “Optical Instruments”). The Group              的光電相關產品的應用領域：如汽車、VR╱ 增
focuses on the optoelectronic-related products that combine optics,            強現實（「AR」）、機器人及手機。

                                                                                   管理層討論與分析


MARKET REVIEW                                                               市場回顧

Looking back to 2023, as the haze of the coronavirus disease 2019           回顧二零二三年，二零一九冠狀病毒病
(“COVID-19”) gradually dissipated, the global economy started the           （「COVID-19」）的陰霾逐漸消散，全球經濟開
process of recovery. Developing economies, in particular, released          始步入復甦進程，尤其是發展中經濟體釋放出
stronger impetus and supported the overall recovery of the global           較為強勁的動能，支撐全球經濟總體向好發
economy. However, the complicated and changeable international              展。然而，國際局勢複雜多變、多個國家通脹
situation, the surging inflation in various countries, the intensified      高企、地緣政治衝突加劇以及全球產業鏈重塑
geopolitical conflicts, the reshaping of global industrial chains and       等考驗仍拖累全球經濟復甦的步伐，增長依然
other challenges still slowed down the pace of global economic              乏力，各行各業都面臨嚴峻挑戰。
recovery. The economic growth remained sluggish and all industries
faced severe challenges.


With the easing of the tight supply of key components, the automobile       隨著關鍵零部件供應緊張得到緩解，汽車生產
production gradually normalized. The global automobile industry             逐漸恢復正常化，全球汽車行業自二零二三年
resumed the strong momentum of growth in mid-2023 and the global            年中開始恢復強勁勢頭，全球輕型汽車的銷量
sales of lightweight vehicles restored the growth trend in 2023. In         在二零二三年重新恢復增長態勢。在這當中，
particular, new energy vehicles have become the key direction for           新能源汽車已然已成為全球汽車產業發展的主
the development of the global automobile industry. According to the         要方向。研究機構EVTank發佈的數據顯示，二
statistics released by EVTank, a research institute, the global sales       零二三年全球新能源汽車銷量達到14,653,000
volume of new energy vehicles reached 14,653,000 units in 2023,             輛，較去年增長約35.4%，其中，中華人民共
representing an increase of approximately 35.4% as compared to that         和國（「中國」）新能源汽車銷量達到9,495,000
of last year. Among them, the sales volume of new energy vehicles           輛，佔全球銷量約64.8%。同時，以智能座艙
in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) reached 9,495,000             及自動駕駛為代表的汽車智能化發展兩大趨勢
units, accounting for approximately 64.8% of the global sales               也催生汽車搭載更為先進的傳感器、控制器及
volume. Meanwhile, the two trends of the intelligent development of         執行器等裝置，並融合更高級別的現代通信及
automobiles represented by intelligent cockpit and autonomous driving       網絡技術，對汽車產業的變革帶來深刻影響。
urged automobiles to be equipped with more advanced sensors,                隨著汽車電動化和智能化進程的日益加速，高
controllers, actuators and other devices and integrated with superior       級輔助駕駛系統（「A D A S」）已基本成為新上
modern communications and network technologies, which will bring            市汽車的核心配置，汽車攝像頭的需求逐漸擴
profound influence on the reform of the automobile industry. With the       大，應用也得以不斷拓展。然而，隨著越來越
increasingly accelerated electrification and intelligence of automobiles,   多新玩家進入汽車攝像頭行業，市場競爭也變
advanced driver assistance systems (“ADAS”) have generally become           得日趨激烈，推動技術創新、提升產品品質以
core configurations of new vehicles. The demand for vehicle cameras         及擴大成本優勢逐漸成為汽車攝像頭供應商鞏
gradually increased and their applications were increasingly expanded.      固及提升市場地位的關鍵因素。
However, with more and more new players entering the vehicle
cameras industry, the market competition becomes increasingly
intensified. Promoting technological innovation, improving product
quality and enhancing cost advantages gradually become key factors
in consolidating and enhancing the market position for suppliers of
vehicle cameras.

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論與分析


Under the impact of the sluggish global economic recovery, the sales            受全球經濟復甦疲弱的影響，VR終端市場的銷
in the VR terminal market were not optimistic. Nevertheless, with the           量不容樂觀，但隨著行業新客戶的加入及更多

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                     股息政策
The Directors consider sustainable returns to the Shareholders to be                董事認為可持續的股東回報是本集團所追求的
one of the main objectives pursued by the Group. The basic policy                   主要目標之一。基本政策是在每個財政年度支
is to pay final dividends in each financial year. Retained profits are              付末期股息。董事會有效利用保留盈利，以為
effectively used by the Board to fund the future development of the                 本集團未來發展提供資金。
Group.


The Directors currently intend to recommend a distribution to all the               董事目前擬建議向全體股東分派相當於各個財
Shareholders in an amount representing approximately 20% to 30% of                  政年度本公司股東應佔年內溢利約20%至30%
the profit for the year attributable to owners of the Company in respect            的款項作為股息。建議股息須由董事會酌情決
of each financial year by way of dividends. The recommendation for                  定。董事會在考慮宣派及派付股息時，會考慮
dividends is subject to the discretion of the Board. The Board takes                以下因素：

THE BOARD                                                                 董事會
Board Functions                                                           董事會職能

Being accountable to the Shareholders, the Board is responsible           董事會須向股東負責，並有責任通過盡責有效
for facilitating the development of the Company’s business by             的方式，推動及領導本公司之業務發展及運
directing and guiding the business developments and operations in a       作，協助本公司業務發展。董事會成員有責任
responsible and effective manner. Board members have a duty to act        真誠、勤勉及審慎工作，維護本公司及全部股
in good faith, with due diligence and care and in the best interests of   東最佳利益。
the Company and the Shareholders as a whole.

executive Directors and managed by the qualified members of the           格管理人員負責管理，董事會負責的決策包
Management, the decisions which are to be made by the Board               括：
include:

     Company’s culture;


4.   Setting performance objectives and monitoring implementation         4.   訂立業績目標及監察實施情況與企業業
     and corporate performance;                                                績；

Board Composition                                                                   董事會組成

The Board should have a balance of skills and experience appropriate                董事會須兼備發行人業務所需的技能及經驗，
for the business of the issuer to ensure that the changes to its                    以確保董事會成員的更替不會造成不必要的業

    devote sufficient time to attend Board meetings, participate in            職引介、培訓及其他與董事會及本公司相

DIVERSITY                                                                 多元化
Board Diversity                                                           董事會成員多元化

The Board has adopted the Board Diversity Policy, the details are set     董事會已採納董事會成員多元化政策，詳情如
out below.                                                                下。

Background and Vision                                                     背景與願景

The Company attaches great importance to the people-oriented              本公司尤為重視以人為本及多元化。本公司明
and diversity in particular. The Company recognises and embraces          白並深信董事會成員多元化對提升董事會履職
the benefits of having a diverse Board to enhance the quality of the      質量裨益良多。
Board’s performance of functions.

Policy Statement                                                          政策聲明

The Company believes that board diversity can enhance the                 本公司相信董事會成員多元化能提高決策能
decision-making capability and a diverse board is more effective in       力，而且一個多元化的董事會能更有效地處理
dealing with organisational changes and would be less likely to suffer    組織的變化，並可較少受到群體思維的影響。
from group thinking. With a view to achieving a sustainable and           為達致可持續及均衡的發展，本公司視提升董
balanced development, the Company sees increasing diversity at the        事會層面的多元化為支持其達到戰略目標及其
Board level as an essential element in supporting the attainment of its   可持續發展的關鍵因素。
strategic objectives and its sustainable development.

In designing the Board’s composition, Board diversity has been            在設定董事會成員組合時，會從不同層面考慮
considered from a wide range of aspects, including but not limited        董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於性別、年
to gender, age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity,           齡、文化及教育背景、種族、專業經驗、營商
professional experience, business perspectives, skills, knowledge         視野、技能、知識及服務任期，以及董事會不
and service term, and any other factors that the Board may consider       時認為相關及適用的任何其他因素，並著重於
relevant and applicable from time to time. High emphasis is placed        確保董事會成員的技能及經驗組合均衡分佈，
on ensuring a balanced composition of skills and experience for the       以提供不同觀點、見解和提問，讓董事會可以
Board members in order to provide a range of perspectives, insights       有效地履行其職務及職責、就本集團的核心業
and challenges that enable the Board to discharge its duties and          務及策略制定良策，以及配合董事會的繼任計
responsibilities effectively, support good decision making in view of     劃及發展。
the core business and strategy of the Group, and support succession
planning and development of the Board.

Risk Management Policy Framework                                                     風險管理政策框架

Effective risk management and internal control systems are critical in               有效的風險管理及內部監控系統對實現本集團
achieving the Group’s strategic objectives. The Group has established                的戰略目標至關重要。本集團已制定風險管理
a risk management policy framework, which was audited and                            政策框架，由董事會及審核委員會審核通過，
approved by the Board and the Audit Committee, and executed by the                   並由風險管理工作小組執行，授權本集團採用
Risk Management Group. It authorizes the Group to take a consistent                  貫徹一致及有效的方針，充分管理與戰略、財
and effective approach applied across the Group to fully manage the                  務、運營、市場及合規相關的風險。
risks associated with strategies, financing, operations, market and
compliance.

To establish the model for the three lines of defense of risk                      根據反虛假財務報告委員會下屬的發起人委員
management according to the integrated framework of the Committee                  會（「COSO」）整合框架，建立風險管理三道防
of Sponsoring Organizations of the Treadway Commission (“COSO”):                   線模型：


First line of defense: Operational departments of each company and                 第一道防線：各公司運營部門及本集團職能部門
functional departments of the Group
The first line of defense mainly conducts collection of initial                    第一道防線主要進行風險初始信息收集和風險
risk information and risk identification, proactively implements                   識別、積極實施風險應對方案，參與風險管理
risk response plans and participates in the construction of risk                   文化建設。
management culture.


Second line of defense: Risk Management Group                                      第二道防線：風險管理工作小組
The second line of defense is mainly responsible for coordinating the              第二道防線主要負責風險管理及內部管控體系
overall planning, construction, maintenance and enhancement of the                 的協調規劃及統籌、建設、維護和提升。
risk management and internal control system.


Third line of defense: Internal audit department                                   第三道防線：內部審計部
The third line of defense is mainly responsible for examining, auditing            第三道防線主要負責對第一及第二道防線的全
and monitoring the overall risk management work of the first line and              面風險管理工作進行稽查、審計和監察。
second line of defense.

Phase 1                                                                   第一階段
Goal Setting                                                              目標設定
The Board establishes common risk assessment criteria and sets up         董事會為本集團設定通用的風險評估準則及制
risk reference tables for the Group. Meanwhile, Risk Management           定風險參照列表。同時，本集團風險管理工作
Group of the Group and Risk Management Group of each business             小組和各事業分部風險管理工作小組討論並確
segment discuss and formulate overall risk management objectives,         定風險管理總體目標、管控要求與組織方式。
management requirements and organization methods.


Phase 2                                                                   第二階段
Risk Identification                                                       風險識別
Each business segment and department collects risk information            各事業分部和部門收集與本集團相關的風險信
related to the Group and identifies the risks that potentially impact     息，並識別對其營運重要程序具有潛在影響的
the key processes of the operations. The Group also identifies            風險。本集團亦識別改進機會。
opportunities for improvement.


Phase 3                                                                   第三階段
Risk Assessment                                                           風險評估
Each business segment and department uses uniform and quantitative        各事業分部和部門運用統一及量化的標準，就
criteria to assess the identified risks along with their impacts on the   已識別的風險及其對業務的影響，以及其發生
business and the possibility of their occurrence to determine the risk    的可能性作出評估，確定風險等級。
level.


Phase 4                                                                   第四階段
Risk Response                                                             風險應對
Each business segment and department determines the overall               各事業分部和部門對評估出的風險確定總體應
response strategy and corresponding response plan for the assessed        對策略及相應應對計劃，實施風險承擔、風險
risks as well as the implementation measures such as risk taking, risk    規避、風險轉移及風險降低等措施以處理風險。
avoidance, risk transfer and risk reduction to deal with the risks.


Phase 5                                                                   第五階段
Control Activity                                                          控制活動
Each business segment and department establishes or modifies              各事業分部和部門建立或修訂相應制度和控制
relevant systems and control activity procedures to ensure that           活動流程，確保採取必要的風險應對和行動計
necessary risk responses and action plans are implemented and the         劃，風險管理應對方案得以執行，以控制和管
risk management response plan is executed, in order to control and        理潛在風險。
manage potential risks.

Group’s Risk Report                                                             本集團風險報告

The Group faces various risks and uncertainties. The relevant key               本集團面對各種風險及不確定因素，有關主要
risks are the risks that the Group must take in order to achieve its            風險乃本集團為實現其戰略目標而必須承受的
strategic objectives. If they are not handled properly, it might bring          風險，倘沒有妥善處理，可能會對本集團的運
adverse impact to the operations of the Group. A comprehensive                  營帶來不利影響。全面的風險評估及風險減緩
risk assessment and risk mitigation measures help ensure possible               措施有助於確保任何潛在嚴重的內部監控缺失
material internal control defects and key risks are properly managed            及主要風險得到適當管理及有效控制，並確保
and effectively controlled, and ensure inside information is properly           根據上市規則妥善處理發佈內幕消息。以下乃
handled and disseminated in accordance with the Listing Rules. The              董事會截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止年度
followings are the principal potential risks that the Board has identified      已識別的主要潛在風險。
for the year ended 31 December 2023.

RESTRICTED SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                       限制性股份獎勵計劃

On 22 March 2010 (the “Adoption Date”), the Board adopted the                       於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採納日期」），董
Restricted Share Award Scheme. Pursuant to the Restricted Share                     事會採納限制性股份獎勵計劃。根據限制性股
Award Scheme, the Directors, all employees, senior staff, agents                    份獎勵計劃，本公司及其附屬公司之董事、全
and consultants of the Company and its subsidiaries are entitled to                 體僱員、高級職員、代理及顧問均有權參與是
participate in this scheme.                                                         項計劃。


The purposes of the Restricted Share Award Scheme are to assist the                 限制性股份獎勵計劃之目的為協助本公司吸納
Company in attracting new staff as well as motivating and retaining its             新人、激勵及挽留現有人才。
existing talents.


The Restricted Share Award Scheme shall be effective from the                       限制性股份獎勵計劃由採納日期起生效，並持
Adoption Date and shall continue in effect for a term of 10 years and               續生效十年，由其管理委員會（「管理委員會」）
be managed by its administrative committee (the “Administrative                     及本公司限制性股份獎勵計劃信託的受託人
Committee”) and the trustee of the Restricted Share Award Scheme                    （「受託人」）管理。於二零二零年三月十六日，
trust of the Company (the “Trustee”). On 16 March 2020, the Board                   董事會決議將限制性股份獎勵計劃的期限延長
resolved to extend the period of the Restricted Share Award Scheme                  十年。因此，本應於二零二零年三月二十一日
for another ten years. As a result, the Restricted Share Awards                     終止的限制性股份獎勵計劃將於二零三零年三
Scheme, which should have originally terminated on 21 March 2020,                   月二十一日終止，除非經董事會決議案提前終
shall, unless terminated earlier by a resolution of the Board, terminate            止。
on 21 March 2030.

The Administrative Committee consists of members of the                            管理委員會由薪酬委員會成員及本公司高級管
Remuneration Committee and senior Management of the Company,                       理層組成，授予董事會管理計劃的權力，包括
delegated with the power an authority by the Board to administer the               根據上市規則第17章審閱及 ╱ 或批准與計劃有
Scheme, including to review and/or approve matters relating to the                 關的事項。

   September 2006 as an exempted company under the Companies                   據開曼群島公司法第 22 章（一九六一年第
   Act Chapter 22 (Law 3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised,                  三條法例，經綜合及修訂，以前稱為公司
   formerly known as Companies Law) of the Cayman Islands and                  法）註冊成立為獲豁免公司，其股份自二零
   its shares have been listed on the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong              零七年六月十五日起在香港聯合交易所有
   Limited with effect from 15 June 2007. Its parent company is                限公司上市，母公司為舜旭有限公司，最
   Sun Xu Limited and ultimate holding company is Sun Ji Limited,              終控股公司為舜基有限公司，兩間公司均
   both are private limited company incorporated in the British Virgin         為於英屬處女群島註冊成立的私人有限公
   Islands. All equity interest of Sun Ji is owned by Sunny Group              司。舜宇集團僱員海外信託擁有舜基的全
   Employee Offshore Trust. Its ultimate controlling party is Mr.              部股權。其最終控股人士為王文鑒先生，

   The Company is an investment holding company. The Company                   本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其

   2.1 Impacts on application of Amendments to                                 2.1 應用香港會計準則第 8 號（修訂
       HKAS 8 Definition of Accounting Estimates                                   本）
                                                                                    「會計估計之定義」的影響

      The Group has applied the amendments for the first time                    本集團於本年度首次應用該修訂本。
      in the current year. The amendments define accounting                      該修訂本將會計估計定義為「財務報表
      estimates as “monetary amounts in financial statements that                中存在計量不確定性之貨幣金額」。會
      are subject to measurement uncertainty”. An accounting policy              計政策可能要求財務報表中之項目以
      may require items in financial statements to be measured in a              涉及計量不確定性之方式計量。在此
      way that involves measurement uncertainty. In such a case,                 情況下，實體會制定會計估計以達致
      an entity develops an accounting estimate to achieve the                   會計政策所載之目標。香港會計準則
      objective set out by the accounting policy. The amendments                 第8號修訂本闡明會計估計變動與會計
      to HKAS 8 clarify the distinction between changes in                       政策變動及錯誤糾正之間的區別。於
      accounting estimates, and changes in accounting policies and               本年度應用該等修訂本並無對綜合財
      the correction of errors. The application of the amendments in             務報表造成重大影響。
      the current year had no material impact on the consolidated
      financial statements.

   2.4 Impacts on application of Amendments to                                 2.4 對應用香港會計準則第1號及香
       HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice Statement 2                                       港財務報告準則實務報告第2號
       Disclosure of Accounting Policies                                         （修訂本） 「會計政策披露」的影
                                                                                   響

      The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in                本集團於本年度首次應用該修訂本。
      the current year. HKAS 1 Presentation of Financial Statements             香港會計準則第 1 號「財務報表列報」
      is amended to replace all instances of the term “significant              修訂以「重大會計政策資料」取代「主
      accounting policies” with “material accounting policy                     要會計政策」一詞的所有情況。倘連同
      information”. Accounting policy information is material if, when          實體財務報表內其他資料一併考慮，
      considered together with other information included in an                 會計政策資料可以合理預期會影響通
      entity’s financial statements, it can reasonably be expected to           用財務報表的主要使用者根據該等財
      influence decisions that the primary users of general purpose             務報表所作出的決定，則該會計政策
      financial statements make on the basis of those financial                 資料屬重大。
      statements.


      The amendments also clarify that accounting policy                        該修訂本亦澄清，即使涉及款項並不
      information may be material because of the nature of the                  重大，但基於相關交易性質、其他事
      related transactions, other events or conditions, even if the             項或情況，會計政策資料仍可屬重
      amounts are immaterial. However, not all accounting policy                大。然而，並非所有與重大交易、其
      information relating to material transactions, other events or            他事項或情況有關的會計政策資料本
      conditions is itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose           身即屬重大。倘一間實體選擇披露非
      immaterial accounting policy information, such information                重大會計政策資料，有關資料不得掩
      must not obscure material accounting policy information.                  蓋重大會計政策資料。

      The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial           綜合財務報表包括本公司、本公司所
      statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                  控制實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。
      Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the                當本公司擁有以下權力時，則視為獲
      Company:                                                                  得控制權：


      •    has power over the investee;                                         •   於投資對象擁有權力；


      •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its              •   因參與投資對象業務而可能或有權
           involvement with the investee; and                                       獲得可變回報；及


      •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.              •   有能力行使其權力影響該等回報。


      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee               倘有事實及情況顯示上述三個控制因
      if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to             素中之一項或多項出現變化，本集團
      one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                將重新評估其是否對投資對象擁有控
                                                                                制權。


      Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains               本集團獲得附屬公司控制權時即對其
      control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses               綜合入賬，而當本集團失去附屬公司
      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses              控制權時，即不再對其綜合入賬。具
      of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are               體而言，自本集團獲得附屬公司控制
      included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and              權之日計起，直至本集團不再擁有附
      other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains                  屬公司控制權之日止，本年度收購或
      control until the date when the Group ceases to control the               出售附屬公司之收入及開支將列入綜
      subsidiary.                                                               合損益及其他全面收益表內。


      Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                溢利或虧損及其他全面收益各項均歸
      are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                    屬於本公司股東及非控股權益。附屬

     Investments in associates                                                 於聯營公司之投資


     An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant            聯營公司乃本集團對其具有重大影響
     influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in           力之實體。重大影響力指參與投資對
     the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but          象財務及營運決策之權力，而並非對
     is not control or joint control over those policies.                      該等政策之控制權或共同控制權。


     The results and assets and liabilities of associates are                  聯營公司之業績、資產及負債使用權
     incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using             益會計法計入本綜合財務報表。以權
     the equity method of accounting. The financial statements of              益會計法處理之聯營公司財務報表乃
     associates used for equity accounting purposes are prepared               按與本集團就於類似情況下之交易及
     using uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for               事件所採用者相同之會計政策編製。
     like transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under              根據權益法，於聯營公司之投資最初
     the equity method, an investment in an associate is initially             乃按成本值於綜合財務狀況表確認，
     recognised in the consolidated statement of financial position            並於隨後就確認本集團應佔該聯營公
     at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                  司之損益及其他全面收益而作出調
     share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive income of             整。損益及其他全面收益外的聯營公
     the associate. Changes in net assets of the associate other               司資產淨值的變動將不會入賬，除非
     than profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not                有關變動導致本集團持有的所有權權
     accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes in                  益發生變更。如本集團應佔聯營公司
     ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s                    之虧損超過本集團於聯營公司之權益
     share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s interest              （包括實質上構成本集團於聯營公司淨
     in that associate (which includes any long-term interests that,           投資之任何長期權益），則本集團會終

       an associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate,           於聯營公司之投資時，投資成本超出
       any excess of the cost of the investment over the Group’s                 本集團應佔被投資方可識別資產及負
       share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and                債之公允值淨額之差額會確認為商
       liabilities of the investee is recognised as goodwill, which              譽，計入投資之賬面值內。倘於重新
       is included within the carrying amount of the investment.                 評估後本集團應佔可識別資產及負債
       Any excess of the Group’s share of the net fair value of                  公允值淨額超逾投資成本，則即時於
       the identifiable assets and liabilities over the cost of the              收購投資期間在損益中確認。
       investment, after reassessment, is recognised immediately in
       profit or loss in the period in which the investment is acquired.


       The Group assesses whether there is an objective evidence                 本集團評估有否客觀證據顯示聯營公
       that the interest in an associate may be impaired. When any               司權益可能減值。如客觀證據存在，
       objective evidence exists, the entire carrying amount of the              則將投資的全部賬面值（包括商譽）作
       investment (including goodwill) is tested for impairment in               為單一資產根據香港會計準則第36 號
       accordance with HKAS 36 as a single asset by comparing                    透過比較其可收回金額（使用價值與公
       its recoverable amount (higher of value in use and fair                   允值減出售成本之較高者）與賬面值測
       value less costs of disposal) with its carrying amount. Any               試有否減值。任何確認之減值虧損不
       impairment loss recognised is not allocated to any asset,                 會分配至任何資產（包括商譽），其屬
       including goodwill, that forms part of the carrying amount                投資賬面值的一部分。倘其後投資的
       of the investment. Any reversal of that impairment loss is                可收回金額增加，則根據香港會計準
       recognised in accordance with HKAS 36 to the extent that the              則第36號確認該減值虧損的撥回。
       recoverable amount of the investment subsequently increases.

     recognised in profit or loss. When the Group retains an                   本集團保留於前聯營公司之權益，且
     interest in the former associate and the retained interest is             保留權益為香港財務報告準則第9號所
     a financial asset within the scope of HKFRS 9, the Group                  界定的金融資產時，本集團於當日按
     measures the retained interest at fair value at that date and             公允值計量保留權益，而公允值則被
     the fair value is regarded as its fair value on initial recognition.      視為首次確認時之公允值。聯營公司
     The difference between the carrying amount of the associate               賬面值與任何保留權益之公允值及出
     and the fair value of any retained interest and any proceeds              售聯營公司相關權益所得任何所得款
     from disposing of the relevant interest in the associate is               項之間的差額，乃計入釐定出售聯營
     included in the determination of the gain or loss on disposal of          公司之損益。此外，本集團將先前在
     the associate. In addition, the Group accounts for all amounts            其他全面收益就該聯營公司確認之所
     previously recognised in other comprehensive income in                    有金額入賬，基準與倘該聯營公司直
     relation to that associate on the same basis as would be                  接出售相關資產或負債的基準相同。
     required if that associate had directly disposed of the related           因此，倘該聯營公司先前已確認其他
     assets or liabilities. Therefore, if a gain or loss previously            全面收益之損益，則會於出售相關資
     recognised in other comprehensive income by that associate                產或負債時重新分類至損益，當有關
     or would be reclassified to profit or loss on the disposal of             聯營公司出售 ╱ 部分出售後，本集團
     the related assets or liabilities, the Group reclassifies the             將收益或虧損由權益重新分類至損益
     gain or loss from equity to profit or loss (as a reclassification         （作為重新分類調整）。
     adjustment) upon disposal/partial disposal of the relevant
     associate.


     When a group entity transacts with an associate of the Group,             倘一間集團實體與本集團聯營公司交
     profits and losses resulting from the transactions with the               易，與該聯營公司交易所產生之損益

     are not related to the Group.                                             報表確認。

       Leases                                                                    租賃


       Definition of a lease                                                     租賃的定義


       A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the           倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
       right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of           內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
       time in exchange for consideration.                                       合約是租賃或包含租賃。


       For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date               就於首次應用日期或之後訂立或修改
       of initial application of HKFRS 16 or arising from business               或由業務合併所引致的合約而言，本
       combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is                    集團根據香港財務報告準則第16 號的
       or contains a lease based on the definition under HKFRS                   定 義 於 初 始 、 修 改 或 收 購 日 期（ 倘 適
       16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as                用 ）評 估 該 合 約 是 否 為 租 賃 或 包 含 租
       appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless                  賃。除非合約的條款及條件其後出現
       the terms and conditions of the contract are subsequently                 變動，否則有關合約將不予重新評估。
       changed.

     Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                   分配代價至合約的組成部分


     For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                 對於包含一項租賃組成部分及一項或
     more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                  多項額外租賃或非租賃部分之合約而
     allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                 言，本集團根據租賃組成部分的相對
     component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of               獨立價格及非租賃組成部分的獨立價
     the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of                格總和，將合約代價分配至各租賃組
     the non-lease components.                                                 成部分。


     The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-                本集團應用實際權宜方法不從租賃組
     lease components from lease component, and instead                        成部分中分拆非租賃組成部分，而是
     account for the lease component and any associated non-                   將各租賃組成部分及任何相關非租賃
     lease components as a single lease component.                             組成部分作為單一租賃組成部分入賬。


     Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                          短期租賃及低價值資產租賃


     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption              對於租期自開始日期起計為12 個月
     to leases of office, staff dormitories that have a lease term             或以內且並無包含購買選擇權的辦公
     of 12 months or less from the commencement date and do                    室、員工宿舍租賃，本集團應用短期
     not contain a purchase option. It also applies the recognition            租賃確認豁免。本集團亦對低價值資
     exemption for lease of low-value assets. Lease payments                   產租賃應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低
     on short-term leases and leases of low-value assets are                   價值資產租賃的租賃付款按直線基準
     recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or another                 或另一系統化基準於租期內確認為開
     systematic basis over the lease term.                                     支。

            particular contract.

     Classification and measurement of leases                                  租賃的分類及計量


     Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as                  本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資
     finance or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease              租賃或經營租賃。當租賃的條款實質
     transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to            上將與相關資產所有權相關的所有風
     ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee, the contract is           險及回報轉讓予承租人時，該項合同
     classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as         被分類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃應
     operating leases.                                                         分類為經營租賃。


     Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit               經營租賃之租金收入於相關租期內以
     or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant            直線法在損益中確認。於協商及安排
     lease. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging         經營租賃時引致之初步直接成本乃加
     an operating lease are added to the carrying amount of the                至租賃資產之賬面值，有關成本於租
     leased asset, and such costs are recognised as an expense                 賃期內按直線法確認為開支。
     on a straight-line basis over the lease term.

       Foreign currencies                                                        外幣


       In preparing the financial statements of each individual                  編製各集團實體之財務報表時，以該
       group entity, transactions in currencies other than the                   實體功能貨幣以外貨幣（外幣）進行之
       functional currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are               交易乃按該交易日期之匯率確認。於
       recognised at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates              報告期末，以外幣計值之貨幣項目以
       of transactions. At the end of the reporting period, monetary             當日之匯率重新換算。按公允值列賬
       items denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at               且按外幣列值之非貨幣項目乃按釐定
       the rates prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items carried             公允值當日之匯率重新換算。當非貨
       at fair value that are denominated in foreign currencies are              幣項目的公允值收益或損失在損益中
       retranslated at the rates prevailing on the date when fair                確認時，該收益或損失的任何交換部
       value was determined. When a fair value gain or loss on                   分亦在損益中確認。以外幣按歷史成
       a non-monetary item is recognised in profit or loss, any                  本計算之非貨幣項目不予重新換算。
       exchange component of that gain or loss is also recognised in
       profit or loss. Non-monetary items that are measured in terms
       of historical cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.


       Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary                貨幣項目結算以及貨幣項目重新換算
       items and on the retranslation of monetary items, are                     所產生的匯兌差額於產生期間的損益
       recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.           確認。


       For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial                 為呈列綜合財務報表，本集團業務的
       statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s operations          資產及負債用本集團採用的呈列貨幣
       are translated into the presentation currency of the Group                （即人民幣）按各報告期末的即期匯率
       (i.e. RMB) using exchange rates prevailing at the end of each             進行折算。收入和費用按期內的平均
       reporting period. Income and expenses items are translated                匯率折算，除非匯率於該期間波動較
       at the average exchange rates for the period unless exchange              大，在此情況下，將使用交易日期的
       rates fluctuate significantly during that period, in which case           匯率。若有任何匯兌差額產生，將於
       the exchange rates at the date of transactions are used.                  其他全面收益中確認及於權益（換算儲
       Exchange differences arising, if any, are recognised in other             備）
                                                                                  （歸屬於非控股權益（如適用））內
       comprehensive income and accumulated in equity under the                  累計。
       heading of translation reserve (attributed to non-controlling
       interests as appropriate).

     (Continued)

     Borrowing costs                                                           借貸成本

     Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,                 因收購、興建或生產合資格資產（即需
     construction or production of qualifying assets, which are                相當長時間方可作擬定用途或出售的
     assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get          資產）而直接產生的借貸成本歸入該等
     ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of            資產的成本，直至資產基本上可作擬
     those assets until such time as the assets are substantially              定用途或出售為止。
     ready for their intended use or sale.


     All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the         所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間於損
     period in which they are incurred.                                        益中確認。

     Government grants                                                         政府補助

     Government grants are not recognised until there is                       政府補助於出現合理保證本集團將會
     reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the                  遵守附帶的條件及收取補助前，均不
     conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be                  會確認。
     received.


     Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                     政府補助於本集團確認該等補助擬作
     a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                    補償之有關成本為開支之期間內按系
     recognises as expenses the related costs for which the                    統基準在損益中確認。尤其是，以本
     grants are intended to compensate. Specifically, government               集團應收購、建造或以其他方式取得
     grants whose primary condition is that the Group should                   非流動資產為主要條件的政府補助，
     purchase, construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets               乃於綜合財務狀況表中確認為遞延收
     are recognised as deferred income in the consolidated                     入，並按系統及合理基準於有關資產
     statement of financial position and transferred to profit or loss         的可用年期內轉至損益。
     on a systematic and rational basis over the useful lives of the
     related assets.


     Government grants related to income that are receivable as                作為已產生開支或虧損之補償或向本
     compensation for expenses or losses already incurred or for               集團提供即時財務支持而無日後相關
     the purpose of giving immediate financial support to the Group            成本而應收與收入有關之政府補助，
     with no future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in          於其應收取期間在損益中確認。此補
     the period in which they become receivable. Such grants are               助呈列於「其他收益」項下。
     presented under “other income”.

       Taxation                                                                  稅項


       Income tax expense represents the sum of the current and                  所得稅開支指即期與遞延所得稅開支
       deferred income tax expense.                                              的總和。


       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the              即期應付稅項根據本年度應課稅溢利
       year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax because of            計算。應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不
       income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                 同，此乃因其他年度的應課稅或可扣
       years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                 稅收支項目，以及毋須課稅或不可扣
       Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates           稅項目所致。本集團的即期稅項負債
       that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end                根據截至報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈
       of the reporting period.                                                  的稅率計算。


       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                       遞延稅項按綜合財務報表中資產及負
       between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the             債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相
       consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax               應稅基之暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負
       bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax             債一般就所有應課稅暫時差額確認入
       liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary            賬。遞延稅項資產一般就所有可扣稅
       differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for             暫時差額確認入賬，惟可能有應課稅
       all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is             溢利用於對銷可扣稅暫時差額。若於
       probable that taxable profits will be available against which             一項交易中，因初次確認資產及負債
       those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such              （業務合併除外）而引致之暫時差額既
       deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the             不影響應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利
       temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other           且於交易時不會產生相等的應課稅及
       than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in              可扣減暫時差額，則不會確認該等遞
       a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the             延稅項資產及負債。此外，倘初步確
       accounting profit and at the time of the transaction does not             認商譽產生暫時差額，遞延稅項負債
       give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary difference.           不會確認。
       In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if the
       temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of
       goodwill.

     differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               公司之投資所產生應課稅暫時差額進
     associates except where the Group is able to control the                  行確認，惟倘本集團能控制暫時差額
     reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that              之撥回，且暫時差額可能不會於可見
     the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable              將來撥回之情況則除外。因有關該等
     future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary             投資的可扣減暫時差額而產生的遞延

     sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of       可見將來撥回時確認。
     the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
     the foreseeable future.


     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the             遞延稅項資產的賬面值於各報告期末
     end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that               進行審閱，並會削減至不再可能有足
     it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be          夠應課稅溢利用於收回全部或部分相
     available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.              關資產為止。


     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax               遞延稅項資產及負債按償還負債或變
     rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the               現資產的期間內預期適用之稅率，根
     liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate          據報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈的稅率
     (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                    （及稅法）計算。
     enacted by the end of the reporting period.


     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects           遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映按照
     the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                 本集團所預期方式於報告期末可收回
     which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period,              或結算其資產及負債賬面值將出現之
     to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and                稅務後果。
     liabilities.

       authority.                                                                對銷。

       except when they relate to items that are recognised in other             與在其他全面收益中確認或直接在權

       the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other                 稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於

     Property, plant and equipment                                             物業、機器及設備


     Property, plant and equipment are tangible assets that are                物業、機器及設備指持作生產或供應
     held for use in the production or supply of goods or services,            貨品或服務或作行政用途（在建工程除
     or for administrative purposes, other than construction in                外）的有形資產，乃按成本減其後累計
     progress, stated in the consolidated statement of financial               折舊及後續累計減值虧損（倘有）於綜
     position at cost less subsequent accumulated depreciation                 合財務狀況表中列賬。
     and subsequent accumulated impairment losses, if any.


     Properties in the course of construction for production,                  用於生產、供應或作行政用途的在建
     supply or administrative purposes are carried at cost, less any           物業以成本減任何已確認的減值虧損
     recognised impairment loss. Costs include any costs directly              列賬。成本包括導致資產處於對於以
     attributable to bringing the assets to the location and condition         管理層擬定之方式營運屬必要的情形
     necessary for it to be capable of operating in the manner                 及條件而直接產生的任何成本（包括測
     intended by management, including costs of testing whether                試 相 關 資 產 是 否 正 常 運 行 之 成 本 ）及
     the related assets are functioning properly and, for qualifying           （對合資格資產而言）根據本集團會計
     assets, borrowing costs capitalised in accordance with the                政策貨本化的借款成本。該等資產按
     Group’s accounting policy. Depreciation of these assets, on               與其他物業資產相同之方式，於資產
     the same basis as other property assets, commences when                   可作擬定用途時開始折舊。
     the assets are ready for their intended use.


     When the Group makes payments for ownership interests of                  當本集團就包括租賃土地及樓宇部分
     properties which includes both leasehold land and building                之物業的所有權作出付款時，租賃土
     elements, the entire consideration is allocated between the               地及樓宇部分的全部代價將以初步確
     leasehold land and the building elements in proportion to                 認時的相對公允值的比例分配。倘可
     the relative fair values at initial recognition. To the extent            合理作出相關款項分配，租賃土地權
     allocation of the relevant payments can be made reliably,                 益於綜合財務狀況表中呈列作「使用權
     interest in leasehold land is presented as “right-of-use assets”          資產」。倘非租賃樓宇部分與相關租賃

     leasehold land, the entire properties are classified as property,

       the relevant cash-generating unit or group of cash-generating

       completion and costs necessary to make the sale. Costs                    本。銷售活動所需成本包括銷售活動

     of the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of                  認。所有以正規途徑買賣之金融資產

     date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or               正規途徑買賣乃要求於市場法規或慣
     sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within          例所確定之時間框架內交付資產之金
     the time frame established by regulation or convention in the             融資產買賣。
     market place.


     Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at      金融資產及金融負債初步按公允值計
     fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts            量，惟根據香港財務報告準則第15 號
     with customers which are initially measured in accordance                 初步計量的與客戶合約產生的貿易應
     with HKFRS 15. Transaction costs that are directly attributable           收款項除外。交易成本直接歸屬於收
     to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial             購或發行金融資產及金融負債（按公允
     liabilities (other than financial assets or financial liabilities at      值計入損益的金融資產或金融負債除
     FVTPL) are added to or deducted from the fair value of the                外），在初始確認時適當地計入或自金
     financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial     融資產或金融負債的公允值中扣除。
     recognition. Transaction costs directly attributable to the               直接歸屬於收購按公允值計入損益的
     acquisition of financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL         金融資產或金融負債的交易成本，即

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating                  實際利率法為計算金融資產或金融負
     the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability            債的攤銷成本以及於相關期間內分配

     the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate              率指在金融資產或金融負債的預計年
     that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                 期內或（如適用）較短期間內準確折算
     payments (including all fees and points paid or received that             估 計 未 來 現 金 收 入 及 支 出（ 包 括 構 成
     form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction         實際利率整體部分的所有已付或已收
     costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected               的費用及點數、交易成本及其他溢價
     life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where             或折扣）至初步確認時的賬面淨值的利
     appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on              率。
     initial recognition.

       Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors                      期預期信貸虧損。預期信貸虧損根據

     (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                    (iii) 信貸減值的金融資產


          A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                   當一件或多件對一項金融資產估計
          events that have a detrimental impact on the estimated                  未來現金流量產生不利影響的事件
          future cash flows of that financial asset have occurred.                發生時，該金融資產則出現信貸減
          Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes             值。金融資產出現信貸減值的證據
          observable data about the following events:                             包括有關以下事項的可觀察數據：


          (a) significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the               (a) 發行人或借款人出現重大財政
              borrower;                                                              困難；


          (b) a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                 (b) 不履行債務或逾期支付等違約
              event;                                                                 行為；


          (c) the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                      (c) 借款人的貸款人因有關借款人
              contractual reasons relating to the borrower ’s                        財務困難的經濟或合約原因，
              financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a                 給予借款人在一般情況下不會
              concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                   考慮的優惠安排；或
              consider; or


          (d) it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                (d) 借款人可能破產或面臨財務重
              bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation.                          組。

       (iv) Write-off policy                                                     (iv) 撇銷政策


            The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                    倘有資料顯示交易對手處於嚴重財
            information indicating that the counterparty is in severe               務困難及無實際期望可收回欠款，
            financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of              本集團則撇銷該金融資產（例如於
            recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been                   交易對手被清盤或進入破產程序
            placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy                 時）。金融資產撇銷仍受限於本集
            proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be                  團收回欠款程序下的執行活動，且
            subject to enforcement activities under the Group’s                     在適當情況下將參考法律意見。撇
            recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice                   銷構成一項終止確認事項，任何其
            where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition              後收回的欠款於損益內確認。
            event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit
            or loss.

     (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


          The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                 預期信貸虧損計量是違約可能性、
          of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the               違約損失率（即發生違約時的損失
          loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.                程度）及違約風險的函數。違約可
          The assessment of the probability of default and loss                   能性及違約損失率的評定乃基於經
          given default is based on historical data adjusted and                  調整歷史數據及前瞻性資料。預期
          forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                 信貸虧損的估算乃無偏頗及概率加
          an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                     權的數額，其按相應違約風險的權
          determined with the respective risks of default occurring               重釐定。本集團經考慮過往信貸虧
          as the weights. The Group uses a practical expedient in                 損經驗後使用撥備矩陣並採用實際
          estimating ECL on trade receivables using a provision                   權益法估計貿易應收款項的預期信
          matrix taking into consideration historical credit loss                 貸虧損，並按無需花費不必要成本
          experience, adjusted for forward looking information that               或精力可取得的前瞻性資料調整。
          is available without undue cost or effort.


          Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                        一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合
          contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                     約應付本集團的所有合約現金流量
          accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                    與本集團預期將收取的現金流量之
          the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective               間的差額，並按初步確認時釐定的
          interest rate determined at initial recognition.                        實際利率貼現。

                                                                                       及

            The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to                    管理層定期檢討分組方法，以確保
            ensure the constituents of each group continue to share                每個分組的組成部分繼續具有類似
            similar credit risk characteristics.                                   的信貸風險特徵。

     received.

       liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company are                 同。本公司所發行的權益工具乃按所

       costs.                                                                    額確認。

       is recognised in profit or loss on the purchase, sale, issue or           行或註銷本公司自有的權益工具概不
       cancellation of the Company’s own equity instruments.                     會於損益中確認收益或虧損。

4. KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION                                                 4. 估計不確定因素的主要來源
   UNCERTAINTY

 In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are             於應用附註3所述本集團之會計政策時，本
 described in Note 3, the directors of the Company are required to            公司董事須就未能於其他來源取得之資產
 make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying                及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及假設。該
 amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent              等估計及有關假設乃根據過往經驗及被視
 from other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions                 為相關之其他因素而作出。實際結果或有
 are based on historical experience and other factors that are                別於該等估計。
 considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
 estimates.


 The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-              估計及相關假設將受到持續檢討。倘會計
 going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in             估計修訂只影響該期間，則有關會計估計
 the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects          的修訂會在修訂估計期間確認，或倘有關
 only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods        修訂同時影響當前及未來期間，則有關修
 if the revision affects both current and future periods.                     訂會在修訂期間及未來期間確認。

   and other key sources of estimation uncertainty at the end of                年度資產及負債賬面值出現重大調整的有
   the reporting period that may have a significant risk of causing             關將來之主要假設及其他估計不確定因素
   a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and                  的主要來源。
   liabilities within the next financial year.


   Provision of ECL for trade receivables                                       貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損撥備

   The Group uses provision matrix to calculate ECL for the trade               本集團使用撥備矩陣以計算並無出現信貸
   receivables which are not credit-impaired. The provision matrix              減值的貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。撥
   is based on debtor’s aging as groupings of various debtors that              備矩陣乃基於債務人的賬齡將具有相似虧
   have similar loss patterns. The provision rate is based on the               損模式的各債務人分組。撥備率乃基於本
   Group’s historical observed default rates taking into consideration          集團過往觀察的逾期率，並考慮無需付出
   the forward-looking information that is reasonable and supportable           過多成本或工作即可取的合理及可支持的
   available without undue costs or effort. At every reporting date,            前瞻性資料。於每個報告日期，過往觀察
   the historical observed default rates are reassessed and changes             的違約率會重新評估，並會考慮前瞻性資

    Credit risk and impairment assessment                                   信貸風險及減值評估

    The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk which will                  本集團承受財務虧損的最大信貸風險
    cause a financial loss to the Group arising from the carrying           來自綜合財務狀況表所列各項已確認
    amount of the respective recognised financial assets as stated          金融資產的賬面值。
    in the consolidated statement of financial position.

    In order to minimise the credit risk, the directors of the              為盡量減低信貸風險，本公司董事已
    Company have delegated a team responsible for determination             委派專責隊伍，負責釐定信貸限額、

      Interest rate benchmark reform                                             利率基準改革


       A fundamental reform of major interest rate benchmarks                    主要利率基準已於全球範圍內進行根
       has been undertaken globally to replace some interbank                    本性改革，以近乎無風險利率替代若
       offered rates (“IBORs”) with alternative nearly risk-free rates.          干銀行同業拆息（「銀行同業拆息」）。
       The Group is closely monitoring the market and managing                   本集團正在密切監察市場及設法向新
       the transition to new benchmark interest rates, including                 基準利率（包括相關銀行同業拆息監管
       announcements made by the relevant IBOR regulators.                       者公佈的利率）過渡。


      HIBOR                                                                      香港銀行同業拆息利率


       While the Hong Kong Dollar Overnight Index Average                        儘管港元隔夜平均指數（「港元隔夜平
       (“HONIA”) has been identified as an alternative to HIBOR,                 均 指 數 」）已 獲 識 別 為 香 港 銀 行 同 業
       there is no plan to discontinue HIBOR. The multi-rate                     拆息利率的替代方案，惟並無終止香
       approach has been adopted in Hong Kong, whereby HIBOR                     港銀行同業拆息利率的計劃。香港一
       and HONIA will co-exist. The Group’s bank borrowings linked               直採納多利率方針，據此，香港銀行
       to HIBOR will continue till maturity and hence, not subject to            同業拆息利率及港元隔夜平均指數將
       transition.                                                               會共存。本集團與香港銀行同業拆息
                                                                                 利率掛鈎的銀行借貸於到期後方會終
                                                                                 止，因此不會受到過渡的影響。

       at amortised cost in the consolidated financial statements                債的賬面值與其公允值相若。
       approximate their fair values.

 The Company adopted The Sunny Optical Technology (Group)               本公司已於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採
 Company Limited Restricted Share Award Scheme (the                     納日期」）採納了舜宇光學科技（集團）有限
 “Restricted Share Award Scheme”) on 22 March 2010                      公司限制性股份獎勵計劃（「限制性股份獎
 (“Adoption Date”) with a duration of 10 years commencing               勵計劃」），該計劃自採納日期起持續十年
 from the Adoption Date. The objective of the Restricted Share          有效。限制性股份獎勵計劃旨在為特選參
 Award Scheme is to provide the selected participants including         與者（包括本公司董事、僱員、本公司及其
 directors of the Company, employees, agents or consultants of          附屬公司的代理或顧問）
                                                                                  （「特選參與者」）
 the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Selected Participants”)         提供一個購買本公司所有人權益的機會；
 with an opportunity to acquire a proprietary interest in the           鼓勵及挽留於本集團工作的個別人士；及
 Company; to encourage and retain such individuals to work with         額外推動彼等爭取達到業績目標，以實現
 the Group; and to provide additional incentive for them to achieve     提升本公司價值，並且透過擁有股份，使
 performance goals, with a view to achieving the objectives of          特選參與者的權益與本公司股東的權益直
 increasing the value of the Company and aligning the interests         接掛鈎的目標。本集團已成立舜宇光學科
 of the Selected Participants directly to be the owners of the          技（集團）有限公司限制性股份獎勵計劃信
 Company through ownership of shares. The Group has set up The          託（「信託」）以管理及持有本公司股份直至
 Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited Restricted            股份歸屬及轉讓予特選參與者。在向特選
 Share Award Scheme Trust (the “Trust”) to administrate and hold        參與者授出股份時，信託在公開市場上購
 the Company’s shares before they are vested and transferred            買將予授出之本公司股份，並由本公司以
 to Selected Participants. Upon granting of shares to Selected          供款方式提供資金。根據限制性股份獎勵
 Participants, the Trust purchases the Company’s shares being           計劃授出的限制性股份由授出日期第一週
 awarded from the open market with funds provided by the                年當日起計至第三到第五（視情況而定）個
 Company by way of contributions. Restricted Shares granted             週年日期間，每個週年日分批歸屬三分之
 under the Restricted Share Award Scheme are subject to a vesting       一至五分之一（視情況而定）。已歸屬的股
 scale in tranches of one-third to one-fifth each (as the case may      份無償轉讓予特選參與者，惟特選參與者
 be) on every anniversary date of the grant date starting from the      須承擔轉讓本公司該等股份應佔或應付的
